Volume - 157 BENGALURU, TUESDAY , 08, NOVEMBER, 2022( KARTHIKA , 17, SHAKAVARSHA, 1944) Issue 224

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 138 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 138 PFH 2022 Dated:29.06.2022
regarding altering the limits of Miragi village of Indi Taluk and Vijaypur District
and constituting a new revenue village called Alamelvasti was published as required
by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964)
in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 138 PFH 2022 Dated:02.08.2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:
03.09.2022 and whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

(16576)

gen Yad BMF UE WUNHS, Honvaed, OG, Adowo’, 9099 OLBLS
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Miragi of Indi taluk, Vijaypur District to form a new revenue village called as
Alamelvasti of Indi taluk, Vijaypur District along with the survey numbers included
in column (4) thereof.

PR-1080
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

No. RD 338 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multistoried Building,
Bangaluru. Date :- 05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka land Revenue
Act, 194 (Karnataka Act No. 12 of 1964) and taking in to consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of Rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience the government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a notification to alter the limits of Chikdinni village of
Manvi Taluk, Raichur District and to constitute a new village called Gali Durgamma
Nagar as specified in schedule along with old survey numbers and new survey
numbers assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 5 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken in to consideration on or after 30 days from the
data of publication of the consideration in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District, Raichur.

SCHEDULE – I

Lands bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Manvi taluk Raichur District to from a new revenue village called as “Gali
Durgamma Nagar” of Manvi taluk Raichur District along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 331 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat

Bengaluru, Date:-05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (1) to (4) of rule 3 of the Karnataka land revenue rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the

village and having regard to the administrative convenience the Government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Desaiboghapur
villages of Maski taluk Raichur district and to constitute a new village called
Shankaralingeswara Nagara as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new survey numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner Raichur District Raichur.
SCHEDULE - 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Desaibhogapur of Maski taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue
village called as Shankaralingeswara Nagara of Maski taluk Raichur district along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 11 GLH 2021 Dated:09-02-2022
regarding altering the limits of Gadisomanal village of Talikoti Taluk and Vijaypur
District and constituting a new revenue village called Vishvanagar and
Sadashivanagar was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No.
RD 11 GLH 2021 Dated:14-02-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:12-08-2022 and whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Gadisomanal of Talikoti Taluk and Vijaypur District to form a new revenue
village called as Vishvanagar of Talikoti Taluk and Vijaypur District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

2022 regarding altering the limits of Handiganur village of Sindagi Taluk and
Vijaypur District and constituting a new revenue village called Bhavaninagar was

affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:04-08-
2022 and whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Handiganur of Sindagi taluk in Vijaypur .District to form a new revenue
village called Bhavaninagar of Sindagi taluk Vijaypur district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 16 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 16 PFH 2022 Dated:- 19-03-2022
regarding altering the limits of Kalakeri village of Talikoti taluk Vijayapur District
and constituting a new revenue village called Jai Sevalal Nagar was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 16 PFH 2022 Dated:28-03-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:- 12-08-2022.

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kalakeri village of Talikoti taluk Vijayapur District to form a new revenue
village called Jai Sevalal Nagar of Talikoti taluk Vijayapur district along with
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 51 PFH 2021 Dated 27-01-2022
regarding altering the limits of Kalagi village of Muddebihal taluk Vijayapur
District and constituting a new revenue village called Ramanadevi Nagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No RD 51 PFH 2021 dated:
31-01-2022 Part 4A Page No 971 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated : 01-08-
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-I

Land bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kalagi of Muddebihala taluk ,Vijayapur District to form a new revenue
village called as Ramanadevi Nagar of Muddebihala taluk ,Vijayapur District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 16 PFH 2022 Dated:- 19-03-2022
regarding altering the limits of Kalakeri village of Talikoti taluk Vijayapur
District and constituting a new revenue village called Jai Sevalal Nagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 16 PFH 2022
Dated:28-03-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:-

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka here by makes the following notification, namely:-

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kalakeri village of Talikoti taluk Vijayapur District to form a new revenue
village called Jai Sevalal Nagar of Talikoti taluk Vijayapur district along with
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1087

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 333 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:.05-11-2022.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (1) to (4) of rule 3 of the Karnataka land revenue rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience the Government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Myadarahal villages
of Maski taluk Raichur district and to constitute a new village called Shri Ramana
Bayi Nagara as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and
new survey numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy commissioner Raichur District Raichur.

SCHEDULE - 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Myadarahal of Maski taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue village
called as Shri Ramana Bayi Nagara of Maski taluk Raichur district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 51 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05/11/2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 51 PFH 2022 Dated:12-04-2022
regarding altering the limits of Nagavi B K village of Sindagi Taluk and Vijaypur
District and constituting a new revenue village called Hanuman nagar was published
as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act
No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 51 PFH 2022 Dated:19-04-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:04-08-2022 and whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Nagavi B K of Sindagi taluk in Vijaypur .District to form a new revenue
village called Hanuman nagar of Sindagi taluk Vijaypur district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1089
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 339 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05-11-2022.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Attanur Village of Sirwar taluk, Raichur District and to constitute a new revenue

village called Subhashchandrabose Nagar as specified in schedule along with maps
with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District, Raichur.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Attanur Village of Sirwar taluk, Raichur District to form a new revenue
village called Subhashchandrabose Nagar of Sirwar Taluk, Raichur district along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 52 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05-11 -2022.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 52 PFH. 2022. Dated:.04-04-2022
regarding altering the limits of Rampura P A village of Sindagi Taluk vijaypur
District and constituting a new revenue village called Rama Nagar was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 52 PFH 2022 dated:13-04-2022 inviting

objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated. 04-08-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Rampura P A of Sindagi taluk in Vijayapur .District to form a new revenue
village called Rama Nagar of Sindagi taluk Vijayapur district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1091

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 340 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:- 05-11-2022.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the
administrative convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent
of residential area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the
boundary of Tungabhadra Village of Raichur Taluk of Raichur District and to
constitute a new revenue village called Ramnagar as specified in schedules along
with maps with old survey numbers and new survey numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of power conferred by section 6 of said act, notice is
hereby given for the information of all persons likely to be affected thereby that the
proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before date specified above will be
considered by the state government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
deputy commissioner Raichur district, Raichur.
SCHEDULE - I
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas

PR-1092

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 337 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Bhootaladinni Village of Sindhanur taluk Raichur District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Raitanagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District, Raichur

SCHEDULE- I

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Bhootaladinni of Sindhanur taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue
village called Raitanagar of Sindhanur taluk Raichur district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 216 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangaluru , Date :05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Edenahlli village of Tiptur Taluk of Tumkuru District to convert the existing
Edenahalli extension unrecorded habitation extend of the existing revenue village.
The area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Edenahlli village for conversion of Edenahalli extension of the said revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act ,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into considaration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the state Government . Objections or suggetion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner , Tumkuru district

SCHEDULE

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Edenahalli extension village of Tiptur
taluk of Tumkuru district and convert the same to form hamlet extention within the
boundary of Edenahlli Revenue village and same will named as Edenahalli
extension of Edenahlli village Tiptur Taluk Tumkuru District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (1) to (4) of rule 3 of the karnataka land revenue rules 1966 such
as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the village and
having regard to the administrative convenience the Government of karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Hadagali villages of Maski

taluk Raichur district and to constitute a new village called Vijayanagar as specified
in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new survey numbers
assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy commissioner Raichur District Raichur.

SCHEDULE - 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Hadagali of Maski Taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue
village called as Vijayanagar of Maski Taluk and Raichur district along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 336 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi storied Building
Bengaluru, Date :05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act. 1964 (Karnataka Act No. 12 of 1964) and taking in to consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience the government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a notification to alter the limits of Hosalli K village of
Sindhanur Taluk, Raichur District and to constitute a new village called Satyanagar
as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new survey
numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by Section 6 of the said, act,
Notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected

thereby that the proposal will be taken in to consideration on or after 30 days from the
date of publication of the consideration in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed
to the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District, Raichur.

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Hosalli K of Sindhanur Taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue
Village Called as Satyanagar of Sindhanur Taluk and Raichur District along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) therof.

PR-1096

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 341 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi storied Building
Bengaluru, Date :05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act. 1964 (Karnataka Act No. 12 of 1964) and taking in to consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience the government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a notification to alter the limits of Jalapur village of
Sirwar Taluk, Raichur District and to constitute a new village called Sreenagar as
specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new survey
numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by Section 6 of the said, act,
Notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken in to consideration on or after 30 days from the
date of publication of the consideration in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed
to the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District, Raichur.
SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing Survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Jalapur of Sirwar Taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue Village
Called as Sreenagar of Sirwar Taluk and Raichur District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) therof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (1) to (4) of rule 3 of the karnataka land revenue rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience the Government of
karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Medikinal villages of
Maski taluk Raichur district and to constitute a new village called Shantinagar as
specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new survey
numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy commissioner Raichur District, Raichur.

SCHEDULE – 1

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Medikinal of Maski Taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue

village called as Shantinagar of Maski Taluk in Raichur district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking in to consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the Administrative convenience and in supersession of
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Samudra village of Devadurga taluk, Raichur district and to constitute a new village
called Suryanayak nagar as specified in schedule alone with maps old survey
numbers and new survey numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected

thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publications of the notifications in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government .Objections or suggestions may Deputy
Commissioner, Raichur District, Raichur.

village called as Suryanayak nagar of a Devadurga Taluk and Raichur District
along with the survey number included in column (4) thereof

PR-1101

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 607 KAMGRARA 2017. Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date: 07-11-2022.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 607 KAMGRARA 2017
Dated:10-08-2017 regarding altering the limits of Bilavaradahalli village of Anekal
taluk Bangalore Urban District and constituting a new revenue village called
Shanubhoganahalli & Kyasaraguppe was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka
Gazette No. RD 607 KAMGRARA 2017 dated:14-09-2017 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:-14-09-
2017.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Bilavaradahalli of Anekal taluk Bangalore Urban District to form a new
revenue village called Shanubhoganahalli of Anekal taluk Bangalore Urban district
along with survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 835 KAMGRARA 2017 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:07-10-2022.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 835 KAMGRARA 2017
Dated:.23-11-2017 regarding altering the limits of Hanagal village of Molakalmuru
taluk Chitradurga District and constituting a new revenue village called
Kommanapatti was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No.
RD 835 KAMGRARA 2017 dated:25-01-2018 inviting objections or suggestions
from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated. 04-04-2018.

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Hanagal of Molakalmuru taluk Chitradurga District to form a new revenue
village called Kommanapatti of Molakalmuru taluk Chitradurga district along with
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

RD 05(10) KAMGRARA 2021 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:07-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 05(10) KAMGRARA 2021
Dated:.14-03-2022 regarding altering the limits of Kallusadarahalli village of
Arsikere taluk Hassan District and constituting a new revenue village called
Gopalapura was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 05(10)
KAMGRARA 2021 dated:15-03-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 30-03-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kallusadarahalli of Arsikere taluk Hassan District to form a new revenue
village called Gopalapura of Arsikere Taluk and Hassan district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Bengaluru, Date:-07-11-2022.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 33 GLH 2021
Dated: 29-01-2022 regarding altering the limits of Dhasihalli village of Arsikere
taluk Hassan District and constituting a new revenue village called Kelagalahatti was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 33 GLH 2021
dated: 02-02-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:-31-03-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Dhasihalli village of Arsikere taluk Hassan District to form a new revenue
village called Kelagalahatti of Arsikere Taluk Hassan district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Bengaluru, Date:07/10/2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 54 PFH 2022
Dated:12.04.2022 regarding altering the limits of Nagavi Village of Gadag Taluk,
Gadag District and to constitute a new village called Shiddarameshwara nagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka
act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No RD 54 PFH 2022 Dated:19.04.2022
inviting Objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the official gazette.

And Where assaid the gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:07.05.2022 and Where as Objections or suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government ;

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification ,namely

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Nagavi of Gadag taluk and Gadag district to form a new revenue village
called as Shiddarameshwara nagar of Gadag taluk Gadag district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 322 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:07-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits Kesturu
Village of Yalanduru Taluk, Chamarajnagar District and to constitute a new
revenue village called K Hosuru as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Chamarajnagar District.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kesturu of Yalanduru Taluk, Chamarajnagar District to form a new
revenue village called K Hosuru of Yalanduru Taluk, Chamarajnagar district
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1109

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 320 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:07/11/2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Bapunagar Village of Chitthapur taluk Kalaburagi District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Dharmanayak Tanda, Devanayak Tanda, Ramunayak
Tanda, Somunayak Tanda, Baddunayak Tanda, Bhojunayak Tanda, Tulasinayak
Tanda and Tukkunayak Tanda as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburagi District, Kalaburgi.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Bapunagar of Chitthapur taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Dharmanayak Tanda of Chitthapur taluk in Kalaburagi district
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

table shall be declared as habitation area of Devanayak Tanda village of Chitthapur

boundary of Dharmanayak Tanda Revenue village and same will herein after be
named as Devanayak Tanda of Dharmanayak Tanda village in Chitthapur Taluk
of Kalaburagi District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of

Harsoor Village of Kalburagi taluk Kalburagi District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Harsoor Tanda as specified in schedule along with maps with
old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalburagi District Kalburagi

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Harsoor of Kalburagi taluk in Kalburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Harsoor Tanda of Kalburagi taluk Kalburagi district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:07-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Ratkal
Village of Kalagi taluk Kalaburagi District and to constitute a new revenue village
called Sevalalnagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburagi District, Kalaburagi.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Ratkal of kalagi taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue village
called Sevalalnagar of Kalagi taluk Kalaburagi district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

Multi storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date :- 07-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act,1964 (Karnataka Act No-12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factor specified in clauses (i) to (vi) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and other head of account and having regard to the administrative

conveniences, the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a notification to alter
the limits of Halyal village of Afzalpur taluk, Kalaburgi district to constitute a new
village Halyal Tanda as specified in now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred
by section 6 of the said act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the
persons likely to be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration
on or after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by state government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state government. Objection oe suggestions may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburgi Dostrict, Kalaburgi.

SCHEDULE-I

Land bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the
areas as specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of
Halyal village of Afzalpur taluk, Kalaburgi District to form a new revenue village
called as Halyal Tanda of Afzalpur taluk, Kalaburgi District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv} of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Niloor village of Afzalpur taluk, Kalaburgi district to constitute a new village
Niloor Tanda as specified in now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by
section 6 of the said act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons
likely to be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by state government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state government. Objection oe suggestions may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburgi District, Kalaburgi.

PR-1115
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 353 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:07/11/2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the
village and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative
convenience the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the
limits of Elenavadagi with its hamlets of Aland taluka, Kalburagi district and to
constitute a new village called Navadagi Thanda in the schedyle.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official gazette.
Any objection or suggeston which may be received by the State
government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the
period specified above will be considered by the State government. Objections or
suggestions may be addressed to the Deputy commissioner, Kalburagi, District,
Kalburagi .
SCHEDULE-I
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
as specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages of Aland taluk, Kalburagi District to form a new revenue village called as
Subhas Nagar of Aland taluk and Kalburagi District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1116

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 352 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:31-10-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Savalagi (K) Village of Aland taluk Kalaburagi District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Savalagi (K) Tanda as specified in schedule along with maps
with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburagi District, Kalaburagi.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Savalagi (K) of Aland taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Savalagi (K) Tanda of Aland taluk Kalaburagi district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act,1964(Karnataka Act No 12 of 1964)and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses(i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and other head of account and having regard to the administrative convenience,
the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a notification to alter the limits of
Benakepalli village of Chincholi taluk, Kalaburgi district to constitute a new village
Phattunayaknagar, Bavanagudinagar as specified in schedule along with maps with
old survey numbers and new survey numbers assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburgi District, Kalaburgi .

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
as specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Benakepalli of Chincholi taluk, Kalaburgi District to form a new revenue
village called as Phattunayaknagar of Chincholi taluk and Kalaburgi District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Bengaluru,Date:07-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Nalwar Village of Chitthapur taluk Kalaburgi District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Ganeshnagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburgi District, Kalaburgi.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Nalwar of Chitthapur taluk in Kalaburgi District to form a new revenue
village called Ganeshnagar of Chitthapur taluk Kalaburgi district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1119
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 220 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru,Date:07-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka land revenue
Act, 1964(Karnataka act no. 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration tha factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village, the Government of Karnataka proposes to

issue a notification to alter the limits of Konchur village of Chittapur taluk,
Kalaburgi district to constitute a new village Diggi Tanda as specified in schedule
along with old survey numbers and new survey numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected there
by that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official gazette.
Any objection or suggeston which may be received by the State government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State government. Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburgi District, Kalaburgi .
SCHEDULE-I
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas as
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Konchur villages of Chittapur taluk, Kalaburgi District to form a new revenue
village called as Diggi Tanda of Chittapur taluk and Kalaburgi District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) there of.

PR-1120
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 345 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:07/11/2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv} of
rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area under land revenue
and having regard to the administrative convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after,
the extent of residential area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the
boundary of Mogala Village of Chittapur Taluk of Kalaburagi District to convert the existing

Mogala Tanda unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing revenue village.
The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified in Schedule
shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of MOGALA village for conversion of
Mogala Tanda unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act, notice is hereby
given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken
into consideration after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government from any persons
with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period specified above will be considered by
the State Government Objections or suggestions may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of
Kalaburagi District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the table shall be
declared as habitation area of Mogala village of Chittapur Taluk of Kalaburagi District convert
the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of Mogala Tanda Revenue village and same
will herein after be named as Guruji Nagar Tanda(Upanagar) of Mogala village in Chittapur of
Kalaburagi District.

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land

factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the
administrative convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent
of residential area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the
boundary of Mogha Village of Kalagi Taluk of Kalburgir District to convert the
existing Sakrunayak Tanda upanagara unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension
/part of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the
village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Mogha village for conversion of Sakrunayak Tanda upanagara unrecorded
habitation in to hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalburagi District.

Taluk of Kalaburgir District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the

Sakrunayak Tanda of Mogha village in Kalagi Taluk of Kalburgir District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Dorjambaga Village of Kamalapur taluk Kalaburagi District and to constitute a
new revenue village called Dorjambaga Nagar as specified in schedule along with
maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburagi District, Kalaburagi

along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1123

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 351 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:07/11/2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv} of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Itakal village of Sedam taluk of Kalaburagi District to convert the existing Alipur
Thanda unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing revenue
village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the schedule
below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Itakal village for conversion of Alipur Thanda unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Allipur Tanda village of Sedam Taluk of
Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary
of Itakal Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Allipur Tanda of
Itakal village in Sedam Taluk of Kalaburagi District.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 12 KAMGRARA 2018 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:07-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 12 KAMGRARA 2018 Dated:
09-02-2018 regarding altering the limits of Chikkagondanahalli village of
Chitradurga Taluk and Chitradurga District and constituting a new revenue village
called Chikkagondanahalli Gollarahatti was published as required by section 6 of
the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the
Karanataka Gazette No. RD 12 KAMGRARA 2018 Dated : 08-03-2018 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 08-04-
2018.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of
Chikkagondanahalli village of Chitradurga taluk in Chitradurga District to form a
new revenue village called Chikkagondanahalli Gollarahatti of Chitradurga taluk
Chitradurga district along with survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1130

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 12 KAMGRARA 2018 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:07-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 12 KAMGRARA 2018 Dated:
09-02-2018 regarding altering the limits of Chikkagondanahalli village of
Chitradurga Taluk and Chitradurga District and constituting a new revenue village
called Chikkagondanahalli Gollarahatti was published as required by section 6 of
the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the
Karanataka Gazette No. RD 12 KAMGRARA 2018 Dated : 08-03-2018 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 08-04-
2018.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of
Chikkagondanahalli village of Chitradurga taluk in Chitradurga District to form a
new revenue village called Chikkagondanahalli Gollarahatti of Chitradurga taluk
Chitradurga district along with survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 11 KAMGRARA 2018 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:07-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 11 KAMGRARA 2018 Dated:
23-01-2018 regarding altering the limits of Sondekola village of Chitradurga Taluk
and Chitradurga District and constituting a new revenue village called
Halagappanahatti was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD

11 KAMGRARA 2018 dated: 29-03-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from all
the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated 29-03-
2018.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of
Sondekola village of Chitradurga taluk in Chitradurga District to form a new
revenue village called Halagappanahatti of Chitradurga taluk Chitradurga district
along with survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.
